[^cite_note-33]: &#x20;Which is not to be conflated with conscription: "impressment describes coercive recruitment performed by police or press gangs. These agents most often targeted men without property, including vagrants and lawbreakers. Impressment did not rely on selecting men from an enrollment list, the act from which the term conscription derived its meaning" (Beattie, Peter M. (2001). *The Tribute of Blood: army, honor, race and nation in Brazil, 1864–1945*. Durham: Duke University Press. Author's note).

[^cite_note-117]: &#x20;[Nunes, Debate teórico e inferências a partir do caso da Doutrina Delta (2023)](#CITEREFNunes,_Debate_teórico_e_inferências_a_partir_do_caso_da_Doutrina_Delta_\(2023\)), p. 65. "Thus, on some topics, the reports of the War Ministers of the 1930s and 1940s differed little from the devastating portrait of the army's situation in 1918, drawn up by the then minister [Pandiá Calógeras](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pandi%C3%A1_Cal%C3%B3geras): failure of the military enlistment and lottery system, little or no military base industry, empty warehouses, scarce and deficient ammunition, mediocre troop instruction, officers more concerned with bureaucracy than missions and professional development". Ferraz, Francisco César Alves (2021). "O Serviço militar brasileiro na hora da verdade: a preparação para o combate em tempos de paz e a participação brasileira na Campanha da Itália". [*Fuerzas Armadas, fronteras y territorios en Sudamérica en el siglo XX: Perspectivas y experiencias desde Argentina y Brasil*](https://www.libros.fahce.unlp.edu.ar/index.php/libros/catalog/view/178/222/1486-1). La Plata: UNLP. pp. 146-147.

[^cite_note-121]: &#x20;The northeastern salient would be the likely [bridgehead](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bridgehead) of a hypothetical [Axis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Axis_powers) invasion of South America, and the Brazilian Army expanded its strength there from 5 to 60 thousand men (Bento, Cláudio Moreira (1991). ["Participação das Forças Armadas e da Marinha Mercante do Brasil na Segunda Guerra Mundial (1942-1945)"](https://ihgb.org.br/publicacoes/revista-ihgb/item/108053-revista-ihgb-volume-372.html). *Revista do Instituto Histórico e Geográfico Brasileiro* (172).). Before Brazil entered the war, the [United States Armed Forces](32212.md) thought about invading the region themselves ([Plan Rubber](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Plan_Rubber)), and did not expect strong resistance from the Brazilian Army in their biggest objective, [Natal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Natal,_Rio_Grande_do_Norte) (Gannon, Michael; Oliveira, José Maria do Amaral (2000). ["Quosque tandem...? Quo jure? A invasão do Brasil"](http://memoria.bn.gov.br/docreader/008567/123121). *Revista Marítima Brasileira*. **120** (10–12).

[^cite_note-163]: &#x20;"In April 1981, following an invitation from the commander of the Argentine Army, general Walter Pires, then Minister of the Brazilian Army, visited military installations in that country and found that the neighboring country, due to the arms race with Chile, had acquired the most modern military materiel, contrasting with the obsolescence of much of Brazilian equipment, especially in terms of artillery, armored equipment and electronic assets". *As forças armadas no Brasil* (1987), Espaço e Tempo, p. 8, cited in Siqueira, Júlio César Aguiar (2019). [*A evolução da estrutura organizacional do Exército Brasileiro entre 1970 e 2005*](https://bdex.eb.mil.br/jspui/bitstream/123456789/5978/1/MO%206130%20-%20AGUIAR%20SIQUEIRA.pdf) (PDF) (Thesis). Escola de Comando e Estado-Maior do Exército. [Archived](https://web.archive.org/web/20221007143423/https://bdex.eb.mil.br/jspui/bitstream/123456789/5978/1/MO%206130%20-%20AGUIAR%20SIQUEIRA.pdf) (PDF) from the original on 7 October 2022. Retrieved 12 January 2024. p 38.

[^cite_note-191]: &#x20;Originally "Strategic Projects" and later "Strategic Programs", grouped into the "Army Strategic Portfolio". See *[O Portfólio Estratégico do Exército](https://ebrevistas.eb.mil.br/VO/article/view/770/816)*, Revista Verde-Oliva, no. 237, 2017.

[^cite_note-207]: &#x20;[Dullius, Gastos militares na América do Sul (2008)](#CITEREFDullius,_Gastos_militares_na_América_do_Sul_\(2008\)), p. 12, noting, however, that the [Colombian Army](2697243.md) was already larger than the Brazilian one. In 2023, however, it had a total personnel of 185,900 ([IISS, The Military Balance (2023)](#CITEREFIISS,_The_Military_Balance_\(2023\)), p. 390), smaller than the Brazilian one.

[^cite_note-208]: &#x20;Marshal, army general, divisional general and brigadier general, respectively identified by five, four, three and two stars ([Hudson, Brazil: a country study (1998)](#CITEREFHudson,_Brazil:_a_country_study_\(1998\)), p. 386).

[^cite_note-209]: &#x20;Colonel, lieutenant colonel and major.

[^cite_note-210]: &#x20;Captains.

[^cite_note-211]: &#x20;First and second lieutenant. The officer candidate, considered a "special enlisted man", is superior to the enlisted men and is part of the junior officers' circle.

[^cite_note-212]: &#x20;Sub-lieutenant, first, second, and third sergeant.

[^cite_note-221]: &#x20;Until then, camouflage was used among paratroopers, jungle troops and cadets. ([Pedrosa, Uniformes do Exército Brasileiro (2022)](#CITEREFPedrosa,_Uniformes_do_Exército_Brasileiro_\(2022\)), p. 734).

[^cite_note-227]: &#x20;[Oliveira & Mathias, Profissionalização militar (2020)](#CITEREFOliveira_&_Mathias,_Profissionalização_militar_\(2020\)), p. 52. The authors only mentioned the sergeants' secondary or technical education, but the [*Escola de Sargentos das Armas*](https://esa.eb.mil.br/index.php/pt/cursos) é of higher education (technologist).

[^cite_note-289]: &#x20;[Santos & Raposo, A elite do Exército (2019)](#CITEREFSantos_&_Raposo,_A_elite_do_Exército_\(2019\)), p. 65-72. Promotions for each position occur at the same time, and student classes trained at AMAN are promoted with a maximum difference of one year between the first and last student. AMAN's classification influences which ones will receive the best opportunities, and the "zero-one" student, the best classified one (oldest) in his respective branch, cadre or service, has a lot of expectations on his shoulders.

[^cite_note-293]: &#x20;[Santos & Raposo, A elite do Exército (2019)](#CITEREFSantos_&_Raposo,_A_elite_do_Exército_\(2019\)), p. 69. The system is strictly "[up or out](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Up_or_out)", in the definitions of [Hudson, Brazil: a country study (1998)](#CITEREFHudson,_Brazil:_a_country_study_\(1998\)).

[^cite_note-314]: &#x20;[Kuhlmann, Razões da permanência do modelo de recrutamento no Brasil (2001)](#CITEREFKuhlmann,_Razões_da_permanência_do_modelo_de_recrutamento_no_Brasil_\(2001\)), p. 72. There are also Medicine, Pharmacy, Dentistry or Veterinary Medicine students who perform mandatory military service as officers, R/2 reserve officers and students from Military Instruction Schools; see the [Military Service Directorate](http://dsm.dgp.eb.mil.br/index.php/pt/o-servico-militar).

[^cite_note-322]: &#x20;For example, through [Citizen Soldier Project](https://dados.gov.br/dados/conjuntos-dados/projeto-soldado-cidadao), existing since 2004 with the objective of "professionally qualifying recruits who provide Military Service, complementing their civic-citizen training and facilitating their entry into the job market, after the mandatory period in the Armed Forces".

[^cite_note-329]: &#x20;Which can be extended for another six months, in the interest of the army. See the [Military Service Directorate](http://dsm.dgp.eb.mil.br/index.php/pt/o-servico-militar).

[^cite_note-334]: &#x20;In other words, less than the ten years necessary for job stability, as provided for by labor legislation; Furthermore, the professionalization of enlisted ranks is traditionally considered within the army as a threat to discipline ([McCann, The Brazilian Armed Forces (2017)](#CITEREFMcCann,_The_Brazilian_Armed_Forces_\(2017\)), p. 75).

[^cite_note-359]: &#x20;"*Braço Forte, Mão Amiga*", established by the Army Social Communication Center in 1992, replacing "Army: Security and Integration" ([Pedrosa, Tradições do Exército Brasileiro (2022)](#CITEREFPedrosa,_Tradições_do_Exército_Brasileiro_\(2022\)), p. 725).

[^cite_note-365]: &#x20;Pedrosa, Fernando Velôzo Gomes (2022). "Bandas, fanfarras, marchas e dobrados militares". [*Dicionário de história militar do Brasil (1822-2022): volume I*](https://books.google.com/books?id=MiGjEAAAQBAJ). Rio de Janeiro: Autografia. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\)) [978-85-518-4909-5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special\:BookSources/978-85-518-4909-5). Music bands have wind, brass, reed, flutes and percussion instruments. The designation of fanfare or band is only formal, as they have the same instruments, just in different proportions. The army does not have a tradition of marching bands, composed only of brass and percussion instruments, as in the [Brazilian Marine Corps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brazilian_Marine_Corps).

[^cite_note-367]: &#x20;"War cries and choruses are used to awaken the enthusiasm of troops in combat situations, sporting events and in their daily lives. Within units or when several units are gathered, they serve to mark the presence of a certain troop element and create an atmosphere of positive rivalry". "It is common for an army soldier to receive an order and respond "Brazil!" or "Jungle!" or "Steel!". Pedrosa, Fernando Velôzo Gomes (2022). "Gritos e refrões de guerra do Exército Brasileiro". [*Dicionário de história militar do Brasil (1822-2022): volume I*](https://books.google.com/books?id=MiGjEAAAQBAJ). Rio de Janeiro: Autografia. [ISBN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISBN_\(identifier\)) [978-85-518-4909-5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special\:BookSources/978-85-518-4909-5).

The Border Infantry Brigade, mentioned in the book, became the Pantanal Infantry Brigade in [2022](https://www.planalto.gov.br/ccivil_03/_ato2019-2022/2022/decreto/D11007.htm).

The [Army's Strategic Conception](https://www.defesanet.com.br/terrestre/exercito-brasileiro-concepcao-estrategica/) of 2017 had only two groups, the strategic deployment and general deployment forces, in addition to the specialized modules.
